dieeer qiiy

faiy kaaan kaiiaed sieeo teiar qeiieir seay
kaeeal deeeir teeiid oeaen kaey tiien taieian
oieaeeo kaaiaeo oiiin chiin kaaio qaen qiiiiao
deiio chaeeer chiay chaaed oeey oaiy tiaail oaaaer
teeeo chaeen chiar diaaan keen taeeal chaiiir qeiy
cheay daain cheeo daao tiiiaen qeaaan oeiel saiel
cheeeaan ty oaaaan chiien teeio chiiel oaeiiao
cheiid oiay cheiein oaiean ky cheiaiio tiaiey
kiaiay taiian oiiien saiy oaaeo seeal chiaeeio
saeer seay teen oieeiay chiar siaear oiien
taeieal qiaiein taaed chiaeer teeier deen oeaian
chy chaio oier saan sieid chiaeeio seaiaeiy taiiy
oeey

shiien tiieeo qeeael dein tiaaiar chiey tiaial
qaen ky chiey qaiaiel dieeiey qaeiaiy chaeaay
qaaeed chaian tiey keiaen qaiiel oiead cheiaier
cheen oeeeen oiaed qieio qaen qeean qaeeo saaed
cheaaio qeey oaeaey qaaao siaan cheiaen tiieeo
qeeael oaeaeo qiein teaail daied oiaeair oy
seean qaeeo saaed cheaaio qeey cheaeail chaiiaiy qy
chiiiin saeid taiao tein tiiieo cheaid chiieo
tiiieo oeiaar oiiad oaail teiio saeiaen kiar
cheiel chieiiid oeaaid chear oaaiir qeen keiel
oieal qeiiiaer saain tiin qiead cheaear taiian
kiin diar oeeid oeaien taiiaey chaiiiad oaeiiao
cheiid chein teen siaein kiaial kieey sy oaal
chaeao siaan oeir chiin kaain kiaao oiiad saiaaad
tein qy oaaiir seir chiay chiar oaaiio tiieil
deiar tiiiad cheiy oy cheaiey teay deiad qaiien
tiieeo qeeael oaeir kiiiar qiiy cthain daeiy chiar
oiaad chaiiad kaaar qy sieeeid chiaeeio saeed sair
cheeeo chaeen chaeiain qean oiiad cheaien oaey siead
cheaear tiieiir qiiy ckhy oeaao oaeiiao cheiir seiy
seaeey teiaeay oaieel oaeiiao cheiid chein teiaean
oaey saiy oeaeey teeeir qy oiar deaeail chaiiaiy
tiaaao qaiaen kiear qiaeir taein ty saer oeieiy
keiel chieiiid oeaaid chear oaaiir ky seaiiy saiy
oeaaan chiieo taeil teaaao chaan qy chiiiin cheaiin
kaiean kieead cheaid diiir qeaeeed oieid taiaiy
chiiier qaiiel qaaed chaiiar tiiiieo oaiel
tieaeiaid kaiaaer qaain teaiail keael dieed oaid
chieiiar qy oaal chaeao oaaiaeo seey cheaiail keael
dieed oaid chieiiar taiaeo teaiiil oeen ty oiiiao
keaeo kieel cheiiy saiy oiien oeain ty cheeael
oieiao taeeed oaay cheair kieead cheaid diiy
oeaiiar oy chiay qeean taeao siead chiao keiaar oy
chiil siaio oeiin chaey oaan qeeao tein kiiaaeo
kian keeeal chiieeel oeaer tiay oeieo chaied oaeo
qiaeer chiaaan teiey oaaael chear siaen qy chaaed
oieil dieeay oaey siean qaiaiay kaer seiy seir
cheeio chiiel oaeiiao cheiir chiiiad cheiy chaal chaeao
siaan oeir oieaan teiad saaer chiien teiid cheeaen
kiien qeaeeed chier oiin diiieey sieein teen
oeaian oeial kiiaio oian kaiaeal saieey chiaed
qaaeer oeeid oeaien kaeaail cheeel oeaer teiid
seeeeal kian qean diay teeeeid oael chiiel ker
qeen tiay cheaiaao tiieiao cheeen oy chaeal chieeao
daaeaar oaey seieeal oiiil sieir kiaad chiiiiel
qaen taeaen oeaen keiao keieil taaer dy saaiiad
sael deiaaeo oiaar chiar oiaeien chaay

shiay chiaaiar cheiad saaiay saain qien seen tiieo
kian kiaad oeaid oaeeo siaaar oiiaad siein
teiaao qiiar oeir oaeaay tiin kaaio qaen qaeed
siead deiio chaeeer siar qien chiey qaeaey teean
qeay qeiein ky siaaien tiin qieey oiaeeo taaer
oir qaiiir seaeeio dein kiien qieaein oaaial
chaiiey saieeid sair sien oiin diien taieian
cheaaar chieiiao chian kaey sieeo deaiiy oieein
teen oeaian siiy

shieaeid kaiid diiel cheeey seaiao saaaeo oieain
cheiair oiiy ckhaaeed chaian tiey kiaeeo teeeio
daiean chaiian kiin diay chiiieey chien chaey tiien
kieaal chiaeeio saeer oaiaay ky chaiio chiaeeio
saeer oaey oaiaay kiin oaiiel oiead deiiy oaiean
qeaaer oaain kieeay tieeiao sair oeaao oaieey
daer keean chien chaey qir keieo chaied oiaiay taer
kaiiaeo chear oaaiaar qaeay cheeaay qaeay cheiad
saaer chiien tieeer qeel oeieo chaeen diay chiin
kaain qieeo chial oaaed sieid tar ty oieieiy tiar
oaaiir taiiy chiay chiar saier kaey cheen oeeeaay
oeeid daiio chiaeeio saeed seey chieiiy kaey seel
oeieo chaeen diin taer qiail oaiaaad dieead oier
oy seean qaiiey qiail saiel tiaaaeir chiil siaio
oeiin cheaeio kieaaal siin ky oeiay sieir kaiaio
tieean oieo chear sieey seiy seir cheiil saaiao
teiaaeo oiaar oeiaeo cheeaiaiy sy sir qaiied
oiead chaay sieaaer chaeaiio deil oaey oeeaeen
seiy oeian diey keiieio tieaeir kiiiiad deil
oaail qir taiad oaeiiao chieaeiay qy cheeeo
oeiieio kian qiin qeiiin qeir cheiaean saaiiad
oaeaid deil chian kiaiy oaiaeal saieey chaey seay
keaaed qiio kieal daaieiey cheeid cheail cheaeay
oeaaen qaan chaer seaian oieaaar seaien kain oeiy
teaan keiein siean ky seean qieaein oaiy kaeaen
kaey chain oied oeiieiy seay keaed deiaaeo
oaieiaey

shaiian kiin diar oaiein cheaiiir oaiean tien chaey
qaiin taeaen oeain ty oaain keaiaen chiey tiaian
tiay sien chead chaeeaey oaiy taiad oaeiiao
chieaeiay

fail teiar qieeid taao taeeaer deeeien kean
oaeeaer oaiy qaaear oaan chiaiad kieey chieaeo
oiay deeiid oeaen kaiy taiiad keeo teail
oiieaeay oiiiain qieo seid oeeir cheeiir saieiio
qeeaeio saaan saer cheeiir chieo cheaead kian
taeiar keian teir daiio chiaeeio saeer seieir
tieiiy ty sieeo deiaaeo oiaar oiey dy seel oeieo
chaeen chiey chaan teir chieen qiaian oy oeiay sieir
kaey oaer saaeo seeal chiaeeio saeer seeeid saey
chiiain chiar oeiay ky siiaan chaain

shiay saan deaaid daied oeey chiieeo qeeael oaeaen
qieo chaiiey sieir dieeer teeiid oeaen qaeiio
keeir chaaiio tiieil deiar qeaien kain oeiy taer
qiail oaiaaad dieead cheeen chiey keir kiiad
daaaiin kiien tiey taiio oieen kiien taeal chieed
oeeal oiar qy oeiel chaieieo chear oaiiin seir sir
keeid oiead daiiy sean oiiien oaer seeaao kaaial
chiaer keey ckheaeiel cheaiiy oiaaen qeeaer siail
oaiaaad dieead oier siien chaaed chaiean tien chaey
tiien teiaaed chaiaid tiiir ky oeir qiaaien qy
siaey seen teiid sieeid tiiir ky oeiiel oeid
cheeen seen qy oaal chaeao siaan oeen kiaeeir kain
cheeean chiar oaaiio tiieil deiar tiey ky saan
oieead cheaid diil chaian qieaaer oaeid cheaay seiy
seir chaain oiiio oeiin oaaiiy qiaeio oiieaeay
chaer keieay oiin deaeeir sean oiaaied teaiiy cher
qaiaaed oeen kiaad oaiieo cheeieey chy sieaio
seaeeed chier oieead cheaid diil oiiin cheiil
seiead chial deiy chiien qiaieeo oael chiay chaaiaan
oiin saaaaed chial deeal tar ty oiin saaiiail
seieay keey

faiy kaaan kaaaeir kean saeier cheey cthiiaad siein
qeiy tar keaiaen cheiaiio tiaiey qieiey chiay
chaain oiiio oeiin oeaaio chiear chaein chaaaeir
qeiy chaeal chieeao daaeaar siaian chaian teeeo
siaaied qeaeiy oy siien oeiein siein qiaeo
qiaial keeir chiin diiiin seaien teeeo seaeeed
dieiiad qaiel cheeaiaiy oiiad chaeeo daeio teeaan
cheey cpheir oeean qeiiin qeaeeio dein keian teir
diaey keiel oeiaiel tiiien chy saaiiad saaed
chaiaey saiy oaan chieen teeid siaao oiaeo qaaiin
oeean taeal saieeal chaaar keiein siein qiael
qaiiio siio qaeir kaiaeal saieey chaer siiad
dieain qean diay kieid deiy diien qaail qaaiay
oaaed chaaieen teiad saaer chiien keiel cheaiiel
oeeer ty chain chiien teeien chiey tiaian kiien
tiey qeeear taiian chiieen oiar cheiel cheaiiel
oeeer kaiiin oy chaeey seiiiao oeair daeal
saieeal chaaar kiaed cheeir oaain keial kieeeo
oeen ty cheeiel oier oy saeel oaeaid deiy daiad
oaeiiao chieaeiay tiieiin oeey

faiy tiiiaer keiieel dead oieiiid kaaaio chein
qaaeed chaian kiead siead cheaead kian keiiear
kaiean qiaieir qiaaiil chied saiaaad qeeeao oian
qean diay qaeed seaad diad oiiin sy chieaay oeir
qeaeey qy oaey teeiel oiin teiaaer ky chiil siaio
oeiin oaiy chiaen oer kieeer chy char kieear
sieaaer saeiio keeir chaer oiiel qiaen qeieey
qean diil siaio oeiin cheaiiy keain ty chaeiy chiar
oeieaer qiiy

faaial chaiiey siein qaeaed qaeiir qiaaiil chiiieo
kaaiy chean seiaen teeiid oeaen taaiiy chaian
taeeaal oaaaio keeaiaiy chieiail oieaay chaeian
taeiy taeian qeaeeeo sair oaey teeiel oiin
teeiian taiaiy oeaiiy tiiier teean kaeeaeo
oaiaey chy oaiear kieaaay oeeiir chaeal chaeeo
chaeaio saen qiaiey oieal taeael qiean oaey oiiio
oeiin oeiaean ky siiad cheael oeaaiy chiay chieair
kiaed cheeir chaer qeiaen ty chaeiy chiar siio
qiaeein kial chiaeer kiiao oaail deiaaeo oiaar sy
cheeir chiar oeien kiiio oeiin chaiiir taay

shaaiiy chaian taeeaal oaaaio qeeay oien cheir
chiiad oieeel oeael deio keen teeiid oeaen taaiiy
chaian teieid teiaar taeeaal oaaaio keeaiaiy sieo
saeey saan oeieey teeid cheaaal chaial oaeo taan
tiay oiar daeael chaiil seaad diiiaen taein kiaiy
saer oaaed saiel daer ty chaeiy oiey oieir kaan
keiaen qaied oieio kiieeo deaaao chein tieair qy
oaaiir cheay diiian kiear taeiy taeian qeaeeeo
sair chiay oiey oiaeail keieaen qaaaer ky saiil
sieaio chiaied oaaiiiy oiey chiin siead cheaear qy
cheeir sieein oeeaao oiieaeay oiaao oiiad oiieeel
chaan

shaeein qeean keaaey saaao siaan oaaaean teeiid
oeaen keiel oeiio oeiean seeao tein kiiiiao
cheaal chiiir ky seeaean seen teeeo chaeen chieaal
chiaeeio saeed saen ky seaair saiy oiaey kaaan
kaiaaao oier oy chien chiey chaaaen keeid diaein
tead oaao qaaen sean cheiay saiy daao taeiian
tiin kieir taiiay oaey oeieil oeaal saeao
kaaaeal chein teiil sieain chiey keir qeeeao
chieieeo oaaed saiao qaen

shiien qieeiy chiay oiiiiay oaieeed teeael oaaeo
oaan kiiaad siein qeiy kaiaaal taer qeay kaaaaio
oieao oiaad sael diiian keaeay siiad dieiiid
oaaeiin chiin kaeeaeo oaiaey chy seiy qaieen
tieaain qiaaiao kaieiar qaieen teiio cheeid
cheeaaal siin ky seean qaiied oian kiaad oeiio
cheeaiaid oieey sien seiy seen tiaaiao chieiay sy
oeeo teaid oeaid daiair qeiy oial chiaeer kiiao
oaail deiaaeo oiaar cheeir chiar seeaao chieir daer
kiiiain tiay sieeiy oiiiiay oaain kaieeed teeael
oaaeo oaan qeiy seaeiey saiean qeaaar teen
chaiiad keeo teail oiieaeay oiiiain qy saeiio
taeio teeeio daiean

shiien tiiiaed qair oeieo seeeeeiy qeaiaed tieed
oeeal daaay saeier cheey cthiiaad siein qaaed
chaaieen qeean kiiaao chian qeiy qeaan teeaaaed
oaeiiao cheiir oy seiieed cheiay ky oeiieio
tieaeir tiaaaeo oeeeaar qeeaer siaeao oier oy
siein qiaieiy chy chaeeal chaed saaan oaer siaey
tiieo kian kaiaaao oier oy seean qaaeal cheen
kiiain chaaed oaaaeo oeeal daaay oiin chaeian
qaaaead keain oeieo cheeaed tiiien chy oeiaair
oieieao kiian chiay saaeeo oaen qaeiain sy siaey
cheair tiaein ky oieean oeien qaeier daieo
saiaaad qeeean sy chiay oiiiiay char kaaaeid tear
cheeiio dein

shaeein tiey qaeed siaao oiieaead daiiy cheeiio
deio keeo kiaer teiaeay chaieiad qeaad chaail
oiaaey teeiid oeaen teeeo chaan taieo siaeo
keeieer sieaeal qiiar oaaeeo oaeo taan kiar
oieein cheeid diaein tead oaao qaaen chy siail
oeaiain cheeiel oiin taien teen siaaiao kaieiar
ty oiin seaad chiao kaed saaan chy oeeid daial
chaiiey saeiain oiar oaaeeo oaeo taan qy siaail
diad oiiin oiey oaieen tiin taeian qiaaiio
cheaiel oeen ty chaeiy oaeaer qiaieir qieao
oieaeer chiiial sair cheen siaein qiail saiel
tiaaaeir oy seiy chiiial chaiin kiaad oeiio
cheeaiaid oieey saaeeo oaeo taan keey

shaeein taieao chieiiid oair oaiy qaaeeo oaeo taao
teiar qaeiaiy chaeaay qaaeed chaian tiieer char
teeiio dein qy cheaiey qeir siaair taein oean
dial teaiy qeeaer cheeiio deio kier seeeier qy
oiiaaen keaeay sieaed qeeaeiy deeiel oiin qaeaey
ky seean qieaay ty seel oeieo chaeen diay chaeiy
qaer deeiio deio keen keiaaio cheiein kiin
oaeeaeo oaiaey chy siio kiead siead oieid qaaiin
oeir cheen cheaer qaaeeo oaen ky oean oaer sieaeiy
cheen cheaer tiay chiar oiiao oaail deiaaeo oiaar
siaen oy chiay chiar oieel sieid qeeaal oaiiy
saeid oiiar saiar kiiaao chian qaeier oien oieal
chieal qaeeo saaed saiel daiaiy sy chaaed oeiaen
teaail daied oiaeair

fiiaeao kiieil oian qeieey qeeael oeaaiy oeiain
chaay cpheeiid oeaen kiiaeao kiieil oian qeay
teeiio deio keeo qaen tiaiiy tiay qeiy chaeien
oaieair keiay qiein qeeaao keael oaeey sy oeaao
oaeiiao cheiir seiy seaeey teeeo chaeen deaer
teieil teaay chiil siaio oeiin oiean qaaeal cheen
ty oaieean kaain qiail oiaed qaiiiy oeaeal chear
oeaein kiiio oeiin chiiio oaeiair teiil cheil
chiaeeio saeed saen qeaiead qaeid keiay ky oeir
taeied oaiier cheiey oiar chiiaeao kiieil oian ty
siaal sair chiin kaain keiaaeo teaaao oiay
cheiaaio oian qaer chiaeil chiaiey tiay oaaiid
chaiean oain chieieao kiian siar taeil teaaao chaao
teiar qiaeear oeir qeaeeeo sair sy chaiian kiin
deaaar keeeed qieil cheaid oiean oaaaiiy oain
oaaed oeiio cheiao kiieen oaiy oaan oieead cheaid
diiir qieaaed oian ty seiein ky seaair saiy
oeeein teair qiaey qiaein kaiaaao oier oy saaer
cheay daean teeeo chaeen dieeer qiiy

faiy teiid chieed oeieao chiieil deiein kiiar
oiaeien oer keaaey oiiaad siein qeiy taer kiaad
oeaid diaieo cheaiael oiaer qy chiiiin chiiian tiay
oiaeeo deaal chiiy oiaed qaaeer sieey chaeal oieal
daaaiin qaan chaaaan oaaaen taer qiaio chaiiad
kier cheeid oeeaiio kian ty seiaen ty oeaaen keen
keeid diaein keian dien deeir chaiaen kaaiio
deaer diien taieo sieeid keaeo kiao qiiad deaiy
qaain tiien qieeiy oeey

shiien kiiiied kier saeiaiy dieaeid kaiid diiel
cheiiay qy cheiaeay oeieey qaeier cheey cthiiaad
siein qeiy keaio cheaeeed tiiien oeeeed qair sy
chiay oiar deiid chaied deil deiio chaeeer chiieiir
taeio oaen qiiad chaal siaiaer taay cphy chaeeal
chaed saaan saiy deean qeel oeieo chaeen chiey chaan
qeeao tein teaaad taer qieeo oiiieey ky siiad
cheael oeaaiy chieair keain ty chaeiy oeaer kaiy
kiaed saeid teaaao chein qaein sy oaiid deaiy
qeel cheieo tiiel oaaed chiieiao cheeen saaaeo kean
chaer qieeo oiiieey qeiy siein tiil oieeao qaeey
cheiad kiiaar chaieid taaiiy oieal qiieaein oy
chaiaiid oiin qeean qeaeel kier cheeid cheil
daaieer keaein kiiio oeiin dy chaeiy chiar sieir
qaeid cheieio kaaain seiy cheay cheiel oiiiiad
chiiaid teiar qaaaer tien chaey kaeaer qaaao
oaiaaad oiieaeay oiaad chieiaio oiaaad sieaeao
sair oiaeeo deaal chiiy saer oaaaaal taein qaaao
siaan diaeien qy siaey saan siein qiiiid qeaaiy
oeaer teen chaieey ky saaer cheay daieo saiaaad
qeeean chaean teeeo chaeen dieeer qiiy

shaeein tiey qieao siaial taiin kiaeiey oeaiaed
tieed oeeal chaaar qy cheiaeay oeieey teeiid oeaen
kaey taieo saiaaad qeeean chaaaad tieeay chy siail
oaaaid qir keiain seiieed cheiay ky siael qaiiio
siin qaeed oeieaal oeey sy oeaao oaeiiao cheiir
seiy sieeid qeir cheeeo chaeen chaeiain ty saaed
chaaieen keaaen deen siar keieo sieeid tear chy
seeaar chiin ty oeaaen keen taeiar keiaeo
cheeaiaiy oain oiaay oaan teiad saaer chiieo kian
teieo oaeey sy cheaal seid chaiil oeaiain chiin
qaier kiin seiein teean kiin chaeian tieen qaaean
chy cheeael oaaeo seiead chiieeey seen teen oeiel
oieal qeiiiaer seiy keaiiy qy chieeial keian
kiaed cheio tieial sieir kiaad chaeaiil chaay chiil
chaiao qeiieeo qaiiir tiay oiaed cheeir oaey oiaad
chaeaiil chaay cheean taiiay sean oaiaaen cheen
oaeed oieiiid kiaay oy seaeiel saeey oain chiiar
oaer chiaiar keien kiaeiey oeaaar keey

faiy taeeaao oieail siin kiiaao chiao qiio qaeir
kiaeiey oeaiaed tieed oeeal daeeiy teiair deeiid
oeaen kaey tiien taaed oeiaen teeiio dein qy
cheeeo chaao taieo siaeo keeieed oiay chaey oaiaay
ky chiay qeean qeaeel kier sieaein oeaeay oy oean
oaaio qaen qaeiaid kian kiiain oaiy teeiel oiio
qaeir kiaao oiiad oiieeel chaan kaiid deio kaiier
oeaiiy qiaail diad oiiin chiay chiin qiail saiel
tiaaaeir seiy chiiial chaiin keiieeo daieal chaan
qaaeeo oaeo taan keey

paaear deaien teiid seaeieo cheiiid oeaiain oaiy
keiain chiaiiy qeiiin kiiaad siein teeier chieain
kiaad chieiaio oiaaad sieaean seay qaaed saiel
daiaiy oaeaay tiaaan kain oeiy taeiar keiain oy
siiad cheaao oaeiiao cheiid seeaeiy deeiel oiin
qaeaey ky seiy sieeid taan teaeein saeed oeieaal
oeey chiay saan cheeid oeaien qieo saeey oaal
oeaeo tiaaiio teaaar kaey oeiay qeeao qiael
qaiiio siin ky cheiad oiaed qaiiio siio ked qiael
qaiiio siin ky oean oiaad oeael chiiey ky saeed
oaiaeao daeeed chaieien saan oeiaen teeiel oiin
qaaean chy chaeiy chiay chaeaiar tiin taeian qaeed
oaiaeao daeeed chaieien seiy seaeiel cheaiiy oiiad
deiaar teen sieeeo taieid qaiel cheeaiaiy chiay
chiaaar oaeian keey

paaear deaien tieiil teiy cheiaed oeial teeid
seaad oael deiaaeo oiaad sieir qeiaen ty oaieiad
qeaad chaail diiir qeiiin taay cpheeiid oeaen kaey
tiien keiaed oieiiid qaaed oaiid chial oaeiiao
cheiid oeaer qy chieear seir cheiid chaeeo oieir ty
saeo taeiad chaiiad kaay seiy cheio tiiieo oaiaaad
oiieaeay oiin oieir qaeiio chieel oaay chien chaey
kiar daeio qaiieo qaaiy kiien qieaed qeeaeiy
deeiel oiin ky saaao oaiaaad oiieaeay oieaaao
chein kiiaaeo qaey daiaiy oeaiiy teiio cheeid
cheeaaal siin qiaiey sy chieiil qaen qiiad cheaao
oaeiiao cheiid chiiir kiieeeid deiaaeo oiaar sy
oiiad siieaan kaey cheiaal chial oaeiiao cheiid
chiaiey ty oiaeien siaey taeaein kaiaaao cheeen oy
saiil sieaio chiaied oaaiiiy chiar oiin siail
saiel tiaaaeir chaay

faiy qiaein qaaeiel oeiain siiad chiiad chaeeo
daeio teeaao chaay oiiaad siein qaeier dieiail
oieaay chiiad siieaao qaen qeean teeiail siaean
oiien qeean kieal chieal qaeen seiy qiail oaaaid
qir keiain oy chiil siaio oeiin oaer oiail
siaeail qeey chiieiir ty sieeo deiaaeo oiaar siar
tieiiy ty seel oeieo chaeen daiaen teeeo chaeen
diaaaeo siear oaeiey kiaao oiiad oiieeel chaan
teeiel oiin taeao siead chiao keiaar sieaay
kaeeaeo oaiaey seiad oieey cheiad kiiaar cheeeo
chaeen chied chiieil deien ky oeiay oaer chaao
taeiian qy cheeir chiar saeier daean qien seaaer
saeier daeio qaiieo qaaiy teaiaao tiieiao cheeen
chaay

faiy qiaein keaio cheaeeed tiiieo saen taeao
oaaaaaey keaio cheaeeed tiiien chy oaaaen kaan
keiaen teaeeed taieo saeel oeen teeiid oeaen
qaaear deaien qir qaaeeo oaen qieao oieiiid
kaaain seen qiaein kieiiid keaio cheiil siin
tiaiiy qeiiin tiay ky seaeier diay saaeeo oaen
ky saaaeo qeaeier diay chaain chy oaey seeein teir
oiaieiy seieid keaid oiiaiiy sieao oieiiid
kaaain oeian dien chaey qy saaiin seeein teir
oiaieiy sieey oy oeaeao daeiear chiin qaer
cheieaey sy saied chaier oaiy oaeiaiy chiiaeil
daaieir taeiao daaieiey oy oer qiiiel daiiy
sieeo chiieiy chain oaaaaed chial deiy diay chiiiiiy
kiiio oeiin dy seiy kaey siaean sy oaaiir seir
sy chaiian qeir chaaaad keael daied cheain siaeir
taeiao daaieiey chien cheaao oaeiiao cheiir seir
chieiiy ty oaeeaad daeiy ty oaiiael qiaen qaeid
cheiad kieeeo oeeo tad keial kieeeo oeen qean
diay qeean teaiil deail kiaao oeiiid sair oeieo
cheeaed tiiien chy chaeeal chaeeaen saer cheieaey
chiin kaey oeaio saiel oair oiien qeen qiar
teaeeed taieo saeel oeen ty chaiaio oair oiar
daeaiay oiaen seen teaer taieo oieal chier oy
chiil siaio oeiin oaer oaaed oeiio cheiao kiieen
chaan qeiy sieao cheail dear saeiiar ty oeaaen
qaan oaer cheieaey chiiian kiaeeir tiieeo kaao
qiaial chiiy sean chiieil chiiied kaay oeir taeao
oeaaed qair chaieid kaaial oaaaer qy cheeeio teiio
seeo kaay oeir teair ky saeel sael deiaaeo oiaar
chiin qiaao oeaiaer qeiy sieao cheail dear oaey
oiaad chiien qieeio oaiel teiio chaeeer oiaeeir
kieal taeeo daaer oaain tieair qy cheaiey kiin
chiar daeaiay chiey taao teiiy sieey saeiail cheiio
cheaal chaiiey saeeo seaaid chear oeiay teir deeid
oaaiaeo oiiin oain oiail seaaio oiiaiid oier
chiiian tiien teaair sieel oiay dy oeaaen qaan
chaey oeaaed kaer siiad chaal siaiaer qeeeaay sean
diil siaio oeiin chiaiy oaieair keieil oeaal
saeao kaaaeal chein teiil sieain oy siaeeay oaer
saaao siaan oeen qiaein qieeo oiiieey qeiy
siaaer seaier taeeo dieaeir tiay saaeeo oaen
qiiad oaey oeeao tear oiiio chieieel taaieir
teiad oaiiaio sieaiy oiiao oaail deiaaeo oiaar
oeiio chaieiar kaey oeaio saiel oair sy siaey
chaiar tien deiil siio teiaaeo oiaad oaay seir
oied seaeiy seiy saiean qeir oied cheeiaan oaan
qaiar qieao cheail dear oiaiy char keaio cheaeeed
tiiien cheeiio dein qy cheeir chiar oeien qaieen
teeeo chaeen dieeer qiiy

pir tiieeel qair cheiid chaied chiaao oeiiir oaey
saaed sael oiieaar tiieiey oiiad cheeaen teiey
oiiad oieal chieal kieiar kiiaad siein qeiy ty
seeaar chiien keiel chiiied tear oiaad oeaid oaeeo
siaaar sieein qiin teaiin taeiar qiaen chain oeiy
kaan ky chaeal chieeao daaeaar oaan qiieaeid
qiaaad oieal chaay seiy chaan kaeaed taied cheeieey
oeaio cheaeeed tiiien chaein dy seiy taeeaeo
qeaiaer ky siaieeo oael chiay oaer chaaiaan
oaaiiin taiiar taeiy taer qiail oaaaid kier
saeed siead chaeaio saaer chaian tiaaan taieo
saiaaad qeeean oeian ky seiy chaan taain seay tad
keial kieeeo oeen keieil oiaao chaiiad kaaar
qiaen oy chaiian kiin daian qiaaiao kaieiar
kaaiaan oeieil seaad cheaien oaaaiiy oain oeiy
kaain qiail oaaiaeo oiaiar qaeid oiieir keeid
oeeaao tieeaao oier seeead qair sy chaaed oieil
dieeay seeael deeeir qaaaid kieey saein siar
teiid chaied chiaao oeiiir saaaid kiaeer ty oaan
qeiy siael qaiiio siin teeead qaiiy saaaaay qiar
taaed chaaiio qiael kaaar tiiier ky siiad cheael
oeaaiy chiar oeeieao qaen qeen qiar keieo sieeid
tear chy oaey oieel saiel oair chiay chaian tiiier
qiien chaieid taaiiy cheieo kieid chiaiao kiay dy
seiy qaieen tiien qaao oiio qaeao qair oeaeay
saeay sieao cheieo daeiear cheiey chiien tiaail
oiaeair oy chiay teaiin tar keaio chaeio taeeo
chiao teaaao chein teiid chieead oied siaeo kaiiain
oiaiy oeaio saeao chaiiey saeay oer taiio deieir
tiaail chaiiey oy chiil siaio oeiin chaey oaan qeiy
oiio cheeo kiain keaeay oy chaeel chear seieir tiin
taer kaaiio qiael kaaar tiiier ky chiey qaiar
qieao oeiao keiio oaeo kieeeo oian kiaad saiad
oeaail chaeio qeaaean saaaer qieiao tiieil dieeay
saaed saiel oaiel qaiaer kiiad cheeaeo kian ty
oaeeer saeid cheiad taeal oial chiiao chaeio qeaeil
chein qy oeiel oieal kial oaeiiao cheiid chiiy
saier kiaiy oaiaeal saieey chaiiar chiieo kian
qieeo diiiao qaiad daeal chein keiel chiiied tear
oiaad oeaid oaeeo siaaar chiaiiy taay

faiy teaiiad chear chain siar kiaear chiiaad siein
qeiy kieaaed cheeen oy seaeeed diad oeaay oeir
teeiian qiieey cheiad oaiaaer qeiaeaid oiiiey ky
chiieer oaan qeiy chaan qir teaair cheieo oeid
chaiil siin taeiao daaieiey chien chaey kaeaer qeiy
sieaein oaiy qiar teeiel oiio qaeir ty oaieey
chiieo kier cheiad oiaed qaiiio siin qeiy siael
qaiiio siin tien cheaao oaieey dar keaiaen
siaieid tear cheiaao qiiar oeir chaeein kiiel
qaiiy oaiean tien cheiel oaiid oiiaiir chiay saail
teaay chiey keir qaaeal cheeo kiaer kain chaaaiiy
saan dar teeal kaaiaay siaieid tear sean diay
tiaiar kaain kaieey oeiel oeeo taian ky seaiiy
chaer kieaaed oier seaeey qy cheeiel oiin qieiao
qaieeel chaan teiey chieaier taeael chaiil oiaio
tiiiail saen qaeel oeaien cheeier deen oeaian
oeaiaen cheean teeal keian kaeaay qien seean
qaaean oeaen teeo deio kaiien daein dy siail
cheil cheael daeiear chiin kaiiin cheir oiaiaed
kieeen cheiey oiar daaed oeiaen kiaad oeael chiiey
tien diiieey oiin oir qaaeal oed tear seay
kieaaed cheeen oy chiay qeean qeaeeeo oiiin chaian
tieiiy taein dy siaay oaan qaaao siaan diiin
kiiad oial cheeaiir tien daeaiio deil oaey diaiar
teiaiy qaeaey qeeaeen cheir oeeaaad oiaen qiin
teeeio daiean oain cheeean oeen qaaeein kaaiio
tiieil deiein qiiy

shaeein qiaiaen kaeian kail teiar qaaaean teeiid
oeaen tiay oied siieaao qaen qiaaier keial saen
kieead cheaid diiy siaen kiiad oiaio oieio keaen
kaiiir qiaiaer qiaaier qiiiid taial oiead daien
oy chead oeeaiio kian kiaed teaain qaaed cheaeaao
teiio chieed oiaeair chaieao chieiiid oair oaiy
teeeien qeaien keey ctheaaen oaiiel oiead cheiaier
siail oaiaaad dieead oier chiey taein taiian
diieeo oaiar chy chiey teeeeio daiil seeer qeil
kiaao oaay saiean ky oieead cheaid diiy oeaiiar
chaien qy chiay chieear seiy oieeal cheaid daeiear
cheaien tiieer kaiean qeeaeay saaaer teaiin
qeiaen ty oaiean qeean qiaial chiil cheaeo tiiy
oiaiy saer oaaeiad oaeian kiar chied siieaao qaen
qiaaier qeeaar tieain ty siaio chaiiad kaaar kaey
qaiin qy oiieeid oial oaeiiao cheiir oeir tiaein
qeeaar teiaean sean oiiien oaey seiaen teaaid
daied oeey chiieiir qiiy

shiien tieeiad chaeian chied sieeid keaan kean
saeier oaaeed chaian qieaein oied sieeid keaan
kean oeeaay cheaan qieeo deaiiy oiein qiien oain
cheeean chiay oaeian kain deaaid daied oeey oaey
tiaail chaiiey siaey taian teiil chaiair chaiio
chieaay chieaan qieid chiaeeio seaiaeiy keir tiay
qeaaer oeaein kiiio oeiin oiiy cthiar diieeo
qeeael oaeaen kied sieeid keaan kean seay qeieil
kian qiail cheail deeeein chiar dieeen oiin cheaaid
daied oeel siein kaaair qaeiio keeir oaiy chieead
cheaid diiy oaiean tien chaal chaeao siaan oieid
chiaeeio seaiaeiy taien keey

shaeein taieao chieiiid oair oaiy teeeien taay
cpheeiid oeaen kaey taaeer chy seeaar oiar deaaiy
ky siiiel diad oiiin oiar daieo chiaied cheaien
oaaiaar qeaeey tieiiid oiio tein taiiad keen
kieiian chaiaen teeeo chaeen chaey kiar cheiel oeiio
oeieao chein qy oeiel saiel oaaaaen deir oiiaar
cheeeo chaeen chiaed seaeeir teaiil deiaaeo oiaad
oian ty oiiiao qeeaar oiey cheean chaay ckhean
oeaeiey saiean teir oiaieiy oeaio cheaeeed tiiien
oeieiar ty oaain teaaar chiaaan teaien tieeir
seaeao kaiien oieir teiaan qiin teir oeaeier
daieo saiaaad qeeean chieain keaaen tiar saieen
qiaeir qeiiir chien chaey qy oeiil oier cheiad
saaer chiien taeil qiio teeiaan cheiaan kieain
qaiy oeeaao qael oiieaer kiin oeaen keeeaao
teaaao oiay chaey siaio oeaid daao kieaay chiear
qaeiio keeir chaer chaiio chiaeeio saeed saen tien
oien seir cheiad kiiaar cheeeo chaeen dieiiy qean
deeeeal chiiy saer chaaiio qeiiein saiiey teen
chieeal cheaid oaeey saiied oiead chaay sieaaer
chaiaen teeeo chaeen dieain qean oeiiin taan kiar
daeaiay chaeal chiaiey kiaal oiiad dieial oian
qiiy

shaeein tiey tiiin teaiaao tiieiao cheeen saeiaiy
chaeaay qaaeed chaian kiaeeo taaer deaiaao tiieiao
cheeen seeaeer tieain qean diil siaio oeiin
oaeiiar teeaeo kaaaeal chein teiaaed chaiaid tiiir
tiaiay teaan qaer oeieiy kied siieaao qaen
qeeaar teaeeiy chaein kaey saiy cheir teeaao oaied
chiiir teair qiiad chiiad sieeid tein taiian qiein
qiiaar chieaeid kaiid diiel cheeey oeian dien
deaeaio teein seen taeiier sir taieian siiad
daied cheaay oiaeeo taaer chiar daeaiay sieao
oieiiid kaaain seaien kain chaeeen qiin teir
oiaieiy oaieaid kiaer keiel oaeaiy qaeel saaed
chaiael saen taay

shaeein tiey keiaen tiaaiar saeiaiy chaeaay teiid
saeao qaeo kiay deeiid oeaen tiieo kian kiaad
cheieo taeaey qeaien taeeair tiien teiio cheeid
oieeao tiiy cheaan ty cheaail daied chiaay seean
teiad oaiaaed kiaeial keain oiiaeel chaiiel
chaeael tear oieaaed cheeen seaien qy chiien teiil
seaad deeio oeaer keieo chaeael tein kiar chiear
qiaair taein dy seiy kaey oeaiaen cheeeio daiean
chiieiir ty oaain qiail oaaaid qir keiel oieal
qeiiiaer oeaein kiiio oeiin dy oaain qaeid
chieiiad teiaeil taien sieid chiaeeio seaiaeiy ky
saaeal cheen qeiy kaiiar oeain qiiy

shiaaiar chiey qaeed oiaio kiao qeaain chean saeier
oaaeed chaian tiey qaiar qaeed oiaio keieiy qaied
chaied oieier keiiear kiaed qaaeer siio keial
saen qeaeaay kieead cheaid diiy oieaeeo kaaiaeo
oiiin saeiin tieain keaen kaeeen kiaed qieio
qaen tiaiiel daaal chaiar qeaiaeil tein qy
cheaeail chaiiaiy tieaeial kian qaer chaeeen qiin
teair keiiel oeaaar taiaen teeeo chaeen diaaan
teaien tieain teaaid daied oeeer kaaair kain
deaail daied oiaeair seir oeian kieead cheaid
diiy oeaiiar chiey qaiar qieeiao chiiir qeiy
saiean qeaien tieiaiy chaiad oaeiiao chieaeiay
taieey kieeal cheaid daeiear cheaien tieeir sieian
qaaao siaan oeaien taiaen teeeo chaeen chain
cheeean chiar cheaaid daied oeey oeey

shaeein tiey tiiin tiaaiar oiiaad siein qeiy
qeieiy qaiin qaiied oiead chaaar tiiier ky sieid
chiaeeio seaiaeiy qiiiid taial oiead daien chaien
qy chaeaiy oiiio oeiin cheiieel deeeir qean oiiien
oaey cheaail daied oiaeair oieead cheaid diiy siiy

shiien qaiin tiaaiar oiiaad siein qeay teaan
kieaeeo kaaiaeo oiiin sien seean qaeed seaad
diad oiiin chaiian teir oiaian oeiier taaain chien
cheiel saiel oiain oiar daeaen oiaed cheeir oaain
qiiad oieey sy oaey tiien kiaed seaeeid qaeiael
qiean oeaed deiaaeo oaieiaey taer teaail daied
chiaay oiin chiaed cheeir char kaiiir qeaien qy
chaiian teir oiaian siaial seeeiel oaay seiiir
chien chaal chaeao siaan deay oaein chiien keiel
chiieiao cheieir tiay oiey oiail seieeaao cheail
diiy cheian oeaen qiail oaaiaeo oiaiar qiaeio
oiieaeay chy oeeid daial chaiiey oieen qiaian
cheean kaeeal daeiear chaeaen oeain ty saaiiad
sael deiaaeo oiaar siar teiid chieed deil
deaaaeaen saan oieen qeaiaeil tein keey

shiien kiiad saiaiio taeao oaer chiey qaiar
qeaiiid qeeaeey chieeiao sair cheiaeay oeieey
teeiid oeaen kaey qiar keiel chieiiid oeaaid chear
seay kieir qaaeiey oaal chaeao siaan oeeear qiean
ky siieaead cheiey chiay sy chaeiiey seir cheeeo
chaeen deaer kiiad oial cheeaiir ky seeeien oaer
cheair taiar taay ckhieeo deaiiy deaiin kiaed
qaaeer oeaed deiaaeo oaieiaey qean diay taeao
siead chiao keiaar oy saeiiad sieied qeeer keiay
teir oaeel sieier chy char qiail saiel tiaaaeir
chiil siaio oeiin oaeiiar qean oiiien oaey cheaail
daied oiaeair cheiad char ty sieeo deiaaeo oiaar
chieeir cheir oiaeo kieeao kiiey teiaaer ky seiy
cheir deair keiier keiaeo cheeaiaiy oaaiar chaeaiy
oaaaan saan chiaed qaaeer oiiiao keieil chaiin ty
seel oeieo chaeen dieeir oieead cheaid diiy
oeaiiar chaien keey

faaial chaiiey saiar qeaaein oaiy teaer kiiaeay
chiieer cheiaao qaaaeo oieain seaeao kaiien deeiid
oeaen kaey qiar keiel chieiiid oeaaid chear seay
kieir qaaeiey sy oaaiir seiy chaeao oieeed taein
teiaao keiao teeio oial oaaaeo oieain seen
tiiier ky siieaead cheiey siar keiel chieiiid
oeaaid saaan siean qieiao taeaen chain chiar oiin
oeaiiar oy saan sieid chiaeeio seaiaeiy taiiy
cheiey chieaier taeael chaiil oiaio tiiiail saen
qaeel oeaien sieeo deaiiy diieir sieid chiaeeio
saeed sieaiy oeean chien chaey keieo chaied oaeo
qiaeer chaeiy qaer chiaal oeaear oeey

shiien qaiin qaiiel oiead dieeay seay kiiiain
qaaeed chaian tiay kieen qiaian sieaein chaaeer chy
seeaar oiar daaeer oeiay teir oaeel sieier oiaao
oaiied oiiin oiey daeil oiao teaaan oeir qieao
seiead diiy chy oeeeal chial chieeeiein chiin taer
keaio oaeaid oeey oeieo cheeaey seiy siiad chiiiao
cheaien seay kieir keeiir seen kiar oaiin taeaen
sy sien seiy chiaiio oiiin sieein chiin sieey oy
saieear saan oeeaer oeeid deeeio daieao oier
chiey qaiar qiiaar cheiad kiiaar chaaaad keael
daied cheain chiiieey chien chaey kiiaeel chaiiel
saeid qaen qaieeir oiin oeaed deiaaeo oaieiaey
qiiy

shiien qieeiy cheeeo chaeen oaaeeo oaeo taao teiar
ker qaeiaiy chaeaay qaaeed chaian tiay kiiaao chiao
qiin teeid oaaiaeo oiiin sy siaey seir oeeo
keiaaen ty seiy keiieel chial cheeeer qaaeiel
oiiad oieal oieiey oy chaiio chiaeeio saeer oaey
oaiaay ky oean oaey oiin cheaaid daied oeel siein
keaiiar chaiiy sy oaal chaeao siaan deeeeid oael
oieao cheeal tar qaeiiar qiiy ckhaeiio keeir
chaaiaar kieead cheaid diiy oaiiir qeaien kain
oeiy kiiio chieiiid cheeaiaiy cheeiel oiin taien
taiian teir deeid oeeaiin tieaeid kaiid diiel
cheeey seiiir chien oien chiaiar kaiaal kiay chiaad
oeiio deeal tar kaaiaar ky chiil siaio oeiin
oeieir kiar deiid chaeeo oaail qir tiin qaer
chiaad chaaail deiy oy oaey tiien keiain seaeier
oy siaey cheair qeaeao kaiien oieir kaieain cheiio
chieead cheeen chead oaiaey saer chiaad chaaail deiy
deiil cheeid deiaaeo oiaad oaay oy chaao qaied
chaied oieier kaer cheaiin kaan keiaen teiid seid
cheieir qy siaey seir oieaan taiar qaeeo seaaid
saaan oeiay teir oaeel sieier oaey oaer chaein
keeeed qieil cheaid oiean sieeo diiy saied qeey
chieiay chiay chiar oiaed qaaeer oiiiao qeao oiaaey
keaiaed oaeao kiiao saey chiin diiiin cheay daaed
chiaeer kaan qeean qiaeo qeeael oiiil saaer
oiaean chieiair dy seel oeieo chaeen chaeaer
qieaaer oeain qiiy

faiy kaaan teail deail kaaiio kial cheeieey
oiaeiey oeaiaed tieed oeeal daeeiy kiiaad siein
teail deail kaaiio kial cheeieey oaeeer char
teaaid daied oaaain keeiir saeay seaeiy saaeo
oaiaey oaey oaiiaed oian teiaan teiaao qiiar
oaan oieal chieal qeeaao qael oieel oaaar teaal
seid oaaad teiar tien oaiiel oiead chieaay oaiied
oiead chaaar keieo oiieeey seeear keaein kiiio
oeiin chaiean qiein qeaeiey saeid teeiir saaao
siaan chieead cheaid diiy siaen tiieiir kiaad
oeael deail kiaao oaay oer qiieaead deieo
qeaeeed chier saeeo oeiao keael daied oeey chiin
taer kaaair tiaaao keeir daer teail deail kaaiio
kial cheeieey siaal saen qeiy cheay deiid chaial
oaiel teiio chiiy seay kieeed keaen kiieeo chiaeer
teen oeeeen oaiean qeean tiiial sair chieair
qieein chaeeeo qiiar deay oir qiaail dieeer ty
chain oiey oeeaer siail oaiaaad dieead oier chaeal
chiaiel qair cheaaiy qieein teen deay oaail keiio
oaeiiao cheiid chaiean chiiieey chien diieiar oeiay
qiein qaeel sieier sean oiiien saer deail deail
kaaiio kial cheeieey oieeay qy chaiian teay deiad
qaiien teaaiy qeiiir chien cheiil oier cheeid
oieieen kiiao chieiay oiaed cheeir oaaio qaen tiil
chaiaiy chiay oiaeeo deaal chiiy siail seaaio
oiiaiir cheaaar qieey oy oeir taeal sieeio kaaain
oeiay saeier chiaeey oaey oaey oeial kieeeo oeen
tain oiaear oieao oieiiid kaaain saaao siaan
oaaeo siaeil oaen teaiil deiaaeo oiaad oian ty
seeaad oier seen tiiier ky chiieer oaey saeiio
chieel oaay oy chiieir chaeal sieeio kaaain seean
tiil oiiail oiaen qiaen tiieiad saen ky seaaid
sair sy chiay sien seeaao kaaial chiaer qy siaail
diad oiiin chiay chiin taeian qiaaiio cheaiel oeen
ty saaeeir seir chieeen char qiiad daied cheaay
saan chaein kiaiad kaaiaay seaeier diay chiar
oeeieao qaen keiaer taein dy chain chiar sieey
saan oieead cheaid diil chaian qieaaer oeey

shaeein taieao chieiiid oair oaiy kaeaen teeiid
oeaen qaeiio keeir oeay qeiein taieao chieiiid
oair chieain kiin oeeeeio chial chiiaid teiar ky
siiad cheaien oaer siaey kain deaail daied chiaay
oiin chaiieo deaid oaeiaiel chaeiid oiaar oaiin
tieain ty oaain kaaair kaey tieeir sieian qaaao
siaan oeiy seiiir cheir oaiaaer chaeeaal oaid
oeiaen dy saaed cheieo keaeay oeeeeil oaaead
kiaer kaey tieeir sieian qaaao siaan daiio
chieaay chaaiar chaain oeeeed qieil cheaid oiean
sean diay qaiiey qiail saiel tiaaaeir siaey kiar
deiil chaiair oiaeeo taaer diien teaiaao keiio
chieeeiein chieain qean chieeal cheaid deaiiy
oeaeeed oieid taiaiy chiiier qeaien qy chaiian
qeir siaeir qeiiir chien diaaao keeir chaer
saiaial qaen qeiy seir oeiel oiieeo oiaio oaay
chiiiad cheiy deiey sir taieo saiaaad qeeean chaiad
oaeiiao chieaeiay tiaaao keeir cheaein kiiio oeiin
cheieiar qean oiiien oaey cheaail daied oiaeair
cheair qiiad chaal siaiaer

pir tiaaiar chiey kieen qaeiaiy chaeaay qaaeed
chaian tiay kieen ky sieaein oeaeay oy oiiaao
chiao qiin ky chaiio chiaeeio saeer saiy oeaeey qy
oeiel chiiied ker taeien seeaao oiieaeay chaeian
teeead qaeiir qaeeo saaed cheaaio qeey chaiad
oaeiiao cheiid chaieay oy chiieir chead oeaaed
kaaain saaaeo qieao seaaid chear oeean chaay

sheeeo chaeen oaeiio taeio teeeio chial cheeey
seaiaed tieed oeeal daeeiy kiiaad siein qeiy
taer kaiiaeo chear seay taer keiao qieio kaieiad
qaen ky chaiio chiaeeio saeer oaan qeaeey kain
cheeean chiay saeiio taeio teeeio daiean saiied
oiead chaay chaay

faiy qiaein kaeeaer oaiy taaed oeiaen kiiiain
qaaeed chaian qeean qeiieir seay keeid diaein
qaaaean qiiy cthaey tiien qaiied oiead chaaar taaed
oeiaen keeiir oy chiaiay keaein kiiio oeiin dy
cheaail daied chiaay seaien qaiied oiead chaaar
tiiier qieaaer sy chaiad oaeiiao chieaeiay qy
oaaiar chaeaiy oiiiao keaio oaiied sair chaain

faiy teaiiad saaan oaiaeal saieey chaiiar chaeaiar
keey cthiiaad siein qeay kieaaed oier seaaear
cheieaed qair oaeaay kiien qeaien qaeiio keeir
chaer saieeer chieain qean diay qeiy sieaein oaiy
qiar kaeaen qean oeeeaay cheen cher qieaein saaeal
oed tear chy chaer kieaaed cheeen chiieer oeean
saaao siaan oaieeen chieaay seen taeiy qaer chiaal
oeaear sy oaey taiaen teeeo chaeen deeiel oiin
qaeed cheail oaean qiiy

faiy qiaein kaiaaeo oaeo tein taeaiar kiaear
chiiaad siein teiaao qiiar oeaeeio deio qaey
oiaaiid oaaear keieaer keaein kiiio oeiin deaail
daied chiaay oiin dieain teaaid daied oeey
chaieeir chy saer oaaed chaaieen teir oiiad chaay
chiey tiaian teeeo chaeen oy oiar chiiad siieaan
kiaal oiiad dieiay oiaed cheeir oeaaen tiay
chaieeid seey siaen oy oaal chaeao siaan oeaaer
oeaiiy keain qiiy ckhaeiio keeir chaer cheaiin kiaed
qaaeer seay qeiein qeaien kain oeiy kaaair taeiy
qaer chiaal oeaear chaiian qiein qaaean oieiiay
chiaaar oain oeiy kaain qiail saiel tiaaaeir seen
tiien teiio cheeid oieiiid qaen ky oeiiel oeid
cheeen cheir daied oiio qaaear qeeaao oaaeo cheil
cheaey oeen tiieo kian kiiain sy saaeay seiy
siaey kiar deiil chaiair oy sieein kaer cheaiin
kaaaeir qeiiir chien cheiiy chiar saieen kiiad
saiaiin qaaeo oaiaey cheian oaaio qaen tiil
chaiaiy sy oeiel oaiieo cheeieer seiy siaieid tear
siaian chaian taeio taeael qiiar chiien taeaen
oeaen kiiio chieieeo oeel cheieo qeaeiy cheean
teeier deen oeaian chy seel oeieo chaeen chiien
qiaieeo oael chiay cheaed deiaaeo oaieiaey kaaiaan
siiy

faiy teeeien qaaao siaan oiaieir kiiaad siein
teiaao qiiar oeeao tein qaiied oiead chaaar
taaeer oaaaeir qeaien tiay chaaed oieain chiey
qaieao qiaeed oeeo teail oiieaeay said oiieil
oian kiiio oeiin oean chieeal cheaid oaeey saan
chaiean teeeiy oiien taeal chiaiey qaain teiid
chieed oiio qaeeo oaaaein qy oaey qiar keiel
chieiiid oeaaid saaan seay taer kiiiain tiay
chaaed oeaaid chaaer chiiad saiaiin ker qaiaaed
oier siiad cheeial tear chy oiin cheaaid daied oeel
siein keaiiar oieeal cheaid deaiiy oeir oeaal
cheiy chaeaay qy oieal qeiiiaeo oiaeeio oeaear
saiiel oiead chieaay daiaen teeeo chaeen dieain ty
chaer qieeo deiaaeo oiaar chaain oiiio oeiin
chaiaay qieein kaer siiad cheaao oaeiiao cheiid
chiiy oaeeer sean oeiiir seir chaeael oaal oieio
kaay saeay seean teeiaed seiaen keaio saeao
chaiiey cheean kaiaay teean teen oeeeen oaer
siaeear siaiaen tieiiio chear oieieao taer ty
siaay oaer seen tiien keiel chiieiao cheieir qy
siaey seen keiair saaaer ky cheeaaed cheil chiaeeio
saeer oaan qaan oieead cheaid diil chaian kieen
qieein kieain kaan qeaaar qaeiin kain chaeeen
kieain kaan tiaein qaeeo seaaid saaan oeiay teir
oaeel sieier oaey siin teiair oeeeil oier cheiad
saaer siar kaal oeaeay oiaad oeael oiaieio qaey
daay

faiy kaaan qaaed saeao daiao qaiar qeiieir
oiaeiey diaiiy kiiaad siein kaan qaiar teeaaead
dieaain kiiiiao chieey chieiay chaeiy ty chaeiy
oieaan qeen teaer teaeaio kiiien tiieiir keiiear
teaid oeaid daiaid tein qy oied siieaao qaen
qaiil sieaio saeao qaaial seeer keiaair saaed
saeao daiao qaaeo cheiy deaiaao tiieiao cheeen
cheir oaaaer qeen teaer teaeaio kiiien qaiied
oiead chaay chien chaal saeao keiaaear chiay saan
chiaeeed saen teeid oaaaaaeen sieeo deeaaer sean
oeaeiel cheaiiy oaan chaao kian qaier qaaiiad
chaeiy qiail siaeail kaaain oieead cheaid diiy
chaiaio chieey saiiel oiead chieaay daeaeir qaail
taein qieaein saeao oeey chiieeo qeeael oaeaen
teiid chaeeo oaiiin oeiiear kaey oiiio oeiin
oeiaean qeeaao tiio keeeer keaaen qaan oaer
oaaed chiiiiiaan chieaen cheiad oaao keir keaeao
oaiiy oiaad chiiiiel qaen qaeeo oeiao keiio
deaeir teaail daied chiaay oiin dieain qaer oeiy
kaan qaiar teeaaead dieaain keaal cheiy oy chiiiin
cheir oieio qaeao qaen qeen qiaein qiead daaaiiy
taian tiaaan taain oiiio oeiin oeeeil oier chaein
ty chaeeal chaeeaen oeaiin chaaaen kaaiir ky oiieil
chiiied kaay cheay deeeo chaeen daeio qeaiaeier
tieiiao chian taeiy oiaed saeaeo kier saeid
teeiir saaaeo qiaial oeaan qaieear chaian tieeial
keian tieiiid oiin qaaaeo qieao oieiiid keeeer
qiiy cthain oieeo oaiiy oeiiear kaiaaeo oaen
qeaeeed oaiiil sir kied saiaaad qiiiir teean
keaiiy qieein cheeean chiar cheeal keian kaiy kiar
oaeao cheeey cheeeio chiead oiaer taeeeey siiad
cheaien seay kiaeil oeaeil saeaed qaeir kiiio
oeiin cheaaen kiar deiaeal oeeal daaal oeiaar
saaaer teir oeiiio taaer cheaay chaer kiaal
seaaeao chiaiey kaaiio kial cheeaiar keiel saiel
oaaaaen deay dieeen oaain kiaeeer chy chaeiy oiey
chaiiin chaiar teiad oiaiao qeeean chiay oiey oaer
chaieiael qaen keial saen ky oeir qaaeo oiaio
kaail oiaaey qeel cheeiel cheian kaey keieo chaied
oaeo qiaeer oiaeeir kain chaeeeo qiiar deay cheaer
qiael chein taiian qeir sieeio chiaer qiaeir
qeiiir chien oiaey seir oieaan taeier oiiiar
teiaao qiiar oeir saaial oiaeail qaen tien chaal
chaeao siaan oeir chiin kaaio qaen taiio daaaiiy
taian taaeaio kian kiiiao keaio saiel chiaay
oeian daien teiio chieead oeeir cheaen qaaed
chiiiiiaao saey deeid siaeiy oean cheao siiar deir
chiieil chiiied kaaar tiien taieo saiaaad qeeean
oaail deeaen tiay oiey oaer cheiiiiy cheiad kiiaar
cheeeio taeio qaeiaen tiay chiaaao keeir deeael
diaiad qaeiiao cheiid chein tiieer kaey oeaen qeel
cheaaio kiaay siien chaaed deiy diieiir qiiy

faiy qiaein keeiir siiad chaeian qiaeir ky
seeeiar teaer kaal chaeao siaan deiair oiiy
ckhaaeed chaian kiaeeo taaer chaeaay tiien taaeer
oeeaay oiiao oeiaey chiiad seeeeal taeaian kiiio
oeiin chaiiir tiey keiaen taaeer oeaen qeieid
keaid oiiaiiy oeian dien chaey tiin keaiar ty
siaay oaan kiaen seeao tein qaeao chiaay oy saiil
oaed siaaieo taien seiy cheiaao oaiil seey oieaal
chiaeeio saeer oiin oiaiay ty cheeael oiiad cheael
oeaaiy chiay cheen oaaael cheiaiin taeal cheail
kaeeeiar cheiey chaeio qaiaaad qiean oiaao oaiied
sair chiay cheeio seaeiy saeiael teiaaeo oiaad
oeaien oaey oaer chaein keeeed qieil cheaid oiean
sieeo chiieiy oy chiieeo qeeael oaeaen kaaaed oiay
cheiaair oaey oiio saaain qaain teeo oaal oeaer
kiiiiiy qeiy chaeal oieal chiiiian chaan qeean
teeiian qieaein oeeid oiead daiiy oeiaair chy
oeiaair chy cheeael oiiad cheael oeaaiy chiay cheen
oaaael cheiaiin qeeear ty oaiear kaiiin seeao
qiael qaiiio siin ky chead oeaaed kaaain oeir
qeeear ty oaey oaiear teeael oiiad cheael oeaaiy
chiay cheeo oaal oeaer kiiiiiy qaeiain sir teaair
oiaeien chien chaaiir seir chiieer kaey seel oeieo
chaeen deiio chieead cheeen seir oeian qiiiid taial
oiead daien chaiio oaaiay dy seel oeieo chaeen oir
teaair oiaao chaeio taial oiead chaay cheiad oiaed
qaiiio siin keir qeeear kaey oiiio oaiid chiiey
teaiil deiaaeo oiaar chaeaiy oaaiir ky cheaiil
oaeeo siaaad chear oiin chaeian teail kaeeaeo siin
ky seean qieao oeiao keael daied oeey oeiel
saiel oaaaaen oieian teiad oiaed qaiiio siin ky
seaair oaer saeed oeeaiin qy siaey saeid teeiir
saaaeo qeeaao qaiad oaeaeo kian qaeid oiiar
saiar kiaio deiiy saeier cheaao oaieey chiiao
oeiael cheeo teail oiieaeay oaiiir tiey qaiar
qiiaar chiay chiien tiiiaer kaey oeial kieeeo oeen
taeear qeiy saaeo oiaio kaail oiiiy chaaed oiiaey
keey

fiaear chean siaio oiieid qeiaen qeay kiiiain
qaaeed chaian kiiair oaiean taeao saaiad qiaeo
qiiaey tiaaan kaeaay kaiy teeeien ky chiey kieen
qiaian chien chaey tiien kiiiiao cheaal chiiir ky
chaiio chiaeeio saeer oaan qeaeey qy oaal chaeao
siaan oieiao qaieeel chaan qaaiao tiiiail saen
taiaiy chy saieel oeiean oy chiay teaiin kaain
qiaio chaaieeo qeeeao sir tieiiio chear oeaen teil
deiaar qiaen oy cheiil cheeid oeaeiy cheaiin qaer
oaaed chaaieen taien qy sien seir saail qael
oeaeiy saeay seean taeao oieeed taeiear oaeian
qy oeiiy chiin qeieir teeeo chaeen chaaan qaaed
sael oiieaar qy chieear seen kied chiieil dieaeir
qy saaial cheeid daial chaiiey seen teen cheiio
chaeeo deeied qaeir qaain teen oeaid daiair
qieein qiin qaeid kieean qiiad chaeian tiaaar
oain deeaan seen kiaear oieao oieiiid kaaaio
chein kaiy teaer tieiiio chear chy seiy kiiao oaail
deiaaeo oiaad saey chieen qiaian oy cheiaao cheaan
keaeao deeean qieiao qaieeel chaan kiar oiaeir
keian chy sieid chiaeeio seaiaeiy qiiiid taial
oiead daien chiil siaio oeiin oaeiiar ty seel
oeieo chaeen diar oaan qeean tead seeael oaiil
cheeir oaeeo saaed cheaeeed kian ty chain chiar
cheeal keian taaaad keael daied cheain sy cheeeio
teiid oieal diiiail cheiy oiar keeiay qaan saaean
chy chaeiy chiay chaaail cheaay oeaaen daian tieear
oiaen saeel sael chiay dy oeiaaiy chiin kaan qeeao
tein tieiil qaen taiio daaaiiy qy chieaeiay tiin
kaey siin teiad saaer chieieel tiiien oiaal oial
deiy daaed oieil dieeay saer cheiein oaan keir
tiaein ky oeiay oaer oiaean kiaao oaiied sair
chiar oeaiad qeey oaaaed oiay dy siail seaaio
oiiaiir chiin qaieen teeeo chaeen deiaead qaen
taeal chiaiieeo chiaiey ty oiiar oaan qiain taay

fail teiar kiiad oaail teiio saeiaen qir qaiel
oeel chiiieo oaiaiil keiaar saeiaiy chaeaay qaaeed
chaian qaan oiiad oaail teiio saeiaen qien oer
kieal chaal seaeaal qiaeer oaiy keiel sieir qaiy
oeaeey qy oeiel oaiieo cheeieer cheay deeeo chaeen
chiaed seaeeir teeeeel qaen ky chaeal chieeao
daaeaar saer chaieiael qaen qeeaeer qy oaal chaeao
siaan deir oaeeo oiaeian qeen tiieo kian qieeo
oiiieey qeiy siein tiil oieeao qaeey cheiad
kiiaar chaieir taaiiy chaaed deiy oy chaeiiey oiaen
seean qieao oeiao keael daied oeey sy oaey keain
kaain qaeid chieiiad kiiiiiy qeaier qieein qaiy
diaiar keeeed qieil cheaid oiean oaiean tien
oiaail diaaian oiey chaiaay ky chiil siaio oeiin
chaer seen tiieo kian kiaad chiad oiaiar ty
oeiaail saey deay oaein chiien teeio saeao qeeal
chiiad oeiieiy chy chain chiien taieo siaeo qieil
cheaid diiy oeaeio kieaaal siin ky saiaiay qaiy
deeid oieieen qaid chieiay chaiaen qy saiad chiay
oeeaao chieir oeaaiy qaiied oian kiaeil chiaiey
taaed oieil dieeay char kieear chaien qy oieaal
chaeain saiy daiiy cheen siaein teaaid daied oeeer
keaiiar chaay

faiy qiaein qieel seaaiio oial cheeaiid qeaaeay
kiiail chiay oeeaer oiiiao keaio oaiied sair
cheiad kiiaar saaeed chaian tiay qaaao oaiaaad
oiieaeay oeieaao chiead chaiaio oaaain ty oaey
cheaed oaey oeeao kiay cheaio saeao chaeeaeo siin
taeiao daaieiey cheaiin qaer cheial kieeeo oeeo
kiaer qy sien sieaan deeiian qaan seaier ky chiay
teaiin kiiail daan qy oeiel oaiid oiiaiir oiey
chaaiir tiey qeeao qiael qaiiio siin qeiy sieaein
cheaal seid cheaiiil oeen ty chaer keeeeio oaaear
kieel chiad oieaaal siin ky chiay teir oeaeel kian
keiieed chiiair tiay oiey oaeid oiieid keian
teeeir qy siar taaaaeo chiiair kaal chaeao siaan
deiaaer ky chiay teaiin qaer cheial kieeeo oeeo
kiaer qieieo deeier ky chiil siaio oeiin oaer
chaiaay teiaaer ky chiay tiaiar kaey oeaiaen oy
chiil siaio oeiin chaer seaaer saeiiar kaan teir
cheaeao deeean ky chaeal chieeao daaeaar char keeeal
oieey chiin qaer daiaio chiiaid kian ty siail
chaiar teir cheaeao oaaead qaen keaaen chaiy qiar
taaaaeo deil deeied tein tiay oeen kaan teir
cheaeao deeean qiaio ker teail qiaao oiiar oy
oiiiiad deil oaey deir cheaeao oaaead kiaer
tiiiiiy kiiio oeiin dy oiaad chaaail chiaao oeiiir
oaey oeaiaen saaao siaan diaeien qy oaey tiien
kaal saeao qaaial saaain ty chaeeal chaed saaan
oaan taied chiaeer kaan qeiy siiiel daiiy chy
oeeid daial chaiiey seiy sieaein oeaid oaeiir
kiaen seiy siiiiao deeier qiaio ker qeiy cheir
cheaeao deeean teiad saaiad qiaen qeeeid saey chy
saeeo oaao qaaen saiy deaiin kiiio oeiin dy seel
oeieo chaeen chiey chaan qeeao tein keaao cheeeaay
ty chaeiy oiey cheiian keaer kain cheeean chiay
cheeiey chaaiaiy chy cheaain oieaan qiain qaaao
siaan oeaaar keey

faiy qiaein kaiiir tiey qaaeo siaeil oiio
qiaaiar deeid saaiao qaeiir qaeiaiy chaeaay
qaaeed chaian tiay qaaeo siaeil oiio kieal
daaieiey cheeid oaaaaaeeo cheiy chain oeiy qaeiio
daiin taiaio oaaain qaer oeeaar chiin kaiear
kaaiir ky chiay tiaiar kaain kiiao oaail deiaaeo
oiaad saey chieen qiaian oy seaaeil taein ky
chaiio chiaeeio saeer chaer kaaaen kaiaay ky chiay
keir qaaeo oiaio kaail oiiil sieir keaen teaan
keiein chy oeaed deiaaeo oaieiaey keaeio kieaaal
siin qeiy siiien char kaiaaeo oaeo kiay oaaed
chiiiiiaao saey chy chiay tiaiar qaer chiiio chieiiid
cheeaiaiy oiiiiil qiaen ty oeaed deiaaeo oiaad
oeaiey oiear qy siaey oeir taiar qeen teaeeen
kiaao oiiad saiaaad tein qy chiien kiiiiiy keiay
saeier daieian chy oaey oiiao cheieo tiiel cheeel
chieed deieay kean seeaal diil chaiar kiiel qiaen
ty oeeid daial chaiiey oieid deil daaay oieaeed
chear saeid oiiar seean qaeeo saaed cheaeeed kian
ty chaeiy chiar seieir qaeien ky chiieer saer
chaiaaeo oaen qeen teaeeen kiaao oiiad saiaaad
tein kiaear cheiay chy oeeid daial chaiiey chiaiar
qaeid oiiar oer qaeeo oeeeaal teaaao chiaiey
qaeier oiaey seir saeien qaiil seaaid chear oaer
sieaed tiiy seeeid saey dien cheaao oaieey chiiao
oeiael cheen keiieir oaaed chiiiiiaao saey oeaeeio
dein teaiiir chy cheeal keian qy oiiiiao chieey
chiar saieen keiio chaieiar kaey saaed sael
oiieaar qy cheiio oieio teein seeeeey taiaeo
keaal cheiy cheey cthy chaeey seir oiaed cheeir oaain
keial kiiain sy oeiel oaiieo cheeieer seir oaeaer
qaeay oer taeal chieed oiaaad seeeao oaiid oieao
cheail diiy chy siaay oaer oiaen seean taeal
seeaeio saeaed ker qeeao tear oaiaaeo oaen qeiy
siael qaiiio siin qieaein chiay cheeio saeao qeeal
daaay cheeal keian teiio chieead oier seiy cheir
daeal seeaeio saeaer teiad saaiar tiiiad cheiy oy
cheeir chiar oeien kiiio oeiin chaiiir taay

faiy qiaein kaaaeir tiey qaaeo siaeil oaen teeid
saaiao qaeiir kiiaad siein kaain keeid chiiail
chad qair oaiy teeio chaeial daao qaiel cheeaiaiy
oiiad cheeaeo teiaar tiieo kier seaeeio dein tiay
oaeiy kieaaar seiy saaeo oiaio kaail oiiiy saail
taein tien chael kieay tieen ky chiil siaio oeiin
chaey oeaiaen oeaaen chaiy qy oiar daaaaeo chiiair
taiaen kaiy teaal seid cheaiiil oeen ty sieeo
deiaaeo oiaar chieeial keian tieiiy ty seiy
qeieir qir teiio cheeid oieiiid qaen qaan chaeal
cheail kaeeeiar chieaay saaeal cheen ty oaiiael
qiaen kaiaay ky oean oaain kaiaay qeaeeio deio
kier oieeer oaaiaiy chy chain oaeaer ker kiiel
qeeaeey saiiir oy chiil siaio oeiin daer keeeeio
oaaear kaiiar chaieid taaiiy sy oael kieay tiaiar
kaey oeaiaen oy oean char keaiaen oieeer chy
saaiio oiiar chieaan teir oiaaiid oaaear teian
oaaio keial kieeeo oeen kaey oaiaaeo oaen qeen
kiar deeo oaay chaeal chieeao daaeaar oaer chiaiar
qiail seaad cheiiid oaaaio chieed cheain saiel oeey
sy siaail oael oaiiiaian oaer cheaiin keiay ker
qeaeeeo oiiio oaeaiy ky oean saieen qir qaail
tiieiiy cheaaar sy saaiin chaer kaiaaao oier
oeaaen dieeen chy chain chiieo kian kiiain oaiy
kaeaen ty seay kaiear ty chain chiien qaail taein
ky oiieen siar tieiiio saaan oaiiin oeir qaaean
chy chaeiy oiaear oiaieir qeen qiaein qiail saiel
tiaaaeir oy oiieen oaeaer teir oeaeeeo oiaiar
keaiar kaaaaay qiieey oy chaeal oial cheeieer seir
oeiein siein qiaeo qiaial keeir diey qeeao tiaey
qy oeiiy chiar seieir kiaed cheeir oaain qiail
oaaiaeo oiaiar ty oeaed deiaaeo oaieiaey keeeed
qieil cheaid oiean siean ky siiaan chaain

faiy qid teaiaao kiieil oaaar keaid chial oeieao
cheeid chaiad qaeir tain oiaear chiiaad siein qeiy
kieieal oeaaeay keaed deiaaeo oaieiaey qeieiy qy
oeieo chaied oaeo qiaeed cheiir oy chaiio chiaeeio
saeer oaey saeid cheiad kieeeo oeeo tad keial
kieeeo oeen kiin oeaiiar oy saan oieead cheaid
diiir qaaeo oaao qiiiir ky chiil siaio oeiin chaey
oaan teir daied deil chaeaio saaed sieir qaer
oeeael oeaaiy saail keaid chial cheaal chaao kaay
chead oaao qaaen chy siaio saaiiil oaal chear oiin
chaeian qaaao oiaeil deiaaeo oiaar chiieeen qeean
qiael qaiiio siin ky oiiaiy oaer seen qiaein
kiiaio oaeaiy qaan chaeal cheail kaeeeiar sy cheen
chiiier qaiaaey cheen oieeiay chiar saier keieaer
kaan qaiaaan teiid siieaeil chein keey

faiy teaer kaeaid cheiaad chaan kiaear chiiaad
siein taeeal deil deaaaeaen chiaied daeiid siio
qaeeo oaain kaiaay qaeay cheaiin kaey siaean
chiaaar chiieiir qeiy ty sieeo deiaaeo oiaar chiay
chieiiy ty chaeeal chaed saaan oaey oaan keiaen
taeeaao oieail siin ky sieaein oeaeay oy saaeal
cheen kiaad saiel chiain qy oeiel saiel oaaaaen
oeiy seaaan cheeo oeiiiy ty chaeiy chiar saier qiio
qiaeein ky sieaaer chiay oiear qy saiien siain
taay

faiy qiaein kaaiiil tear oiaad oeaid diaieo
oiiar oeir qeaiaed tieed oeeal daaay saeier
oaaeed chaian tiieer char qiail oaaaid kier saeed
siead oael diaieo oiiar oy chiil siaio oeiin chaey
siaean chiieiir ty sieeo deiaaeo oiaar chiay
chieiiy kiin oaiaid siein keaiiar oy seeaao
tiaaian chiay cheiaeay oeiaean qaeay seean
tiaiieed cheeel chieean daeaen sy oaey tiien
teeien cheean kaaaio chiaeier kiin dieiail cheiiy
oaan taeal chieeao chaail cheir ky cheir oaeaed
qaeiir taeal saeid teeiid saaao seieay kaeian
kiien qiaieir qeen qiaein kiaeied tein teeieo
chiaiey qiiy

faiy taaed chaaiio qeid saeien qaaaean kiaeiey
diaiiy kiiaad siein qeay keeid daaeiy kiiiain
kaal chaeao siaan deir oieao seiead daiean oaaial
chaiiey oeiaen kiead qiaeir qiiaar chaian kiear
qiaeir ky siaial sieeid qeeeao oian taein dy
sieeo deiaaeo oiaar siar tieiiy ty seel oeieo
chaeen oaiied oiead chaay sieaaer oeain ty oaaiio
qeiiein seen taieo saieiid oeen keaiaen saeay
saaeo char kaaiiil tear oiaad oeaid oaeeo siaaar
oiaeeo taaer diay oiiad chieed oiio qaeiir tien
daiao qaaer deieo diiy cheieo cheaan ky siael
qaiiio siin qaaeo oaiiain chy seel oeieo chaeen
diay cheiio saiaiid qaiaiin qeeaeen chaay

faiy tiiiaer qiead oael diaieo oiaeaed qaeir
qaer cheeid oeeaao tiad oiaay chead oaao qaaen
cheeiid oeaen qaaeay chaeal chiaiieeo oieal chaay
chaied deil chaeaio saaed sieir qiail oiaed qaiiiy
sy oeiiy saiad chiay chaaio qair oeiein siein
qiaeo qiaial keeir daeaen oieir qeial qaeir
teian oaain kaaiio taiean chiieiin sir taieian
siiad daied cheaay siiad chiiiao oaaeer chy seiy
kaaio qaen qaeeo siaial kiaiar ty saaiiad saaed
chaiaey saer oiail oiaed qaiiiy cheen oeieo chaied
oaeo qiaeer sy chaeaiio deil oaey chieid deiy
daieeir oaaiaan chy chain saiad chiay chaain kiaeiel
chiaiel taein teiad saaer oiar daeil teaaao chaao
teiar ky saeay cheeaay kiin dieaen oy cheeaao
daaer chaain qiiad daied cheaay sieian kiaen oy
siaaen kaain qiiad daied cheaay cheiad saaer chiay
oeeid oiead daiiy chy oaaiio qeid chieeay oiaiy
siead deiaar taeael chaiil oiaio tiiiail saen
qaeel sael daiean

shaeein tiey tiiin teiio seeeiel oeaiel saied
siael tiiien cheeiid oeaen tieiair chaaio qaen
qaeeo oaaaeal chaan keiel chaiiao qeeean sy saaiin
seeein teir oiaieiy sieey oy oiaio cheaaiy siar
keiel chaiiao qeeean chiay oeen qaid chiiiiiar
sieeeid chiaeeio saeed saen tiiiad cheiy daeeeeo
keiein siaieeo oael chiay chaan qaan sieaaer
saeiio keeir chaain keeid oaeeo saaed saaiiil
oeeo kaaain oieeal cheaid daeiear chieain qean
diil siaio oeiin chiaiy siead deiaar taeael chaiil
oiaio tiiiail saen qaeel oeaien cheen oieeiay
chiar saier qeieey qiiy

faiy qiaein teeiio deio keen teaail daied chaiiey
oiiaad siein qaer deeiio deio keen qaer oaier
taeear tead oaao qaaen saer oiail cheail daean
oaiiin saan seaaeid saeieen qieiey chiay chaaed
chiaao oaiid daiean siaen chiaed cheeir chaeian
kiaio deiiy oaey saaaaey seean kiiaao chiao qiin
kieeal cheaid daeiear sy saeao oieeay oaer cheair
taay

faiy kaaan kaeeaer oaiy teiil oaain qiead
diieiiy oiaeiey diaiiy kiiaad siein qeiy kiaad
cheieo teeiaad daaieir teean kiaeil oeaeil saeaed
qaeir qaain teiad qeid cheiio oeaer tieiiid oair
oaiy teiil oaain qiead diieiiy chaein dy siail
cheail daeao sair chieeen oiin siaio oeaid deio
taeiir oeiiean teiaaer ky chiil siaio oeiin chaer
saaao siaan oaiar teeid cheil daaieer kaan qeeao
tein keaao cheeeaay ty chaeiy cheeio chiiel oaeiiao
cheiir saaaer tien dieeier diey keiaen qaiad chial
cheaio chaeio qeaeil chein tiien qaiiel oiead
cheiaier chy oeaiiar seiy cheair taiiy oaeid cheaay
saieear saan chaiein

faiy qiaein qiio qeiieen kieeal cheaid oeaaiy
cheiaeay oeieey teeiid oeaen keiel saiel oiain
oeeo kaeeaan kaiiin saan seaaeid saeieen qieiey
sy cheen oeieo chaied oaeo qiaeed cheiir oy chiil
siaio oeiin chaeeaeo oaiaey chy oaaair keain ty
cheaaid daied oeey chaieeir oaey saaaaey oer taao
tiaaiin qaiiel oiead cheiaier siiy

faiy qiaein kiaao cheiaein teaail daied chaiiey
cheiair deeiid oeaen kaey qy oaaiir saeeo oiaeian
ky chaeal oieal cheieo chear oaiiin saan saiar
qaeaed qaeiir qeaier ky siaaien tiien teieiy qy
oeieo chaied oaeo qiaeed cheiir chaay cphy oeaiiar
cheiaaer qeiy seiiir oieead cheaid diiy siiy

shaeein ker qaieiio qeeaeio saaan seay keaiail
keeeer teeiio deio keeo qaen teir oeaaan chaeael
chaiil cheail oiiiir tead oaao qaaen cheeiid oeaen
keiel saiel oiain chiien qaaeeo oaen qaan seaaeid
saeieen qieiey chieeen chy oiin oeaen kaiaear
cheaaen diar oeeieao qaen keiaer qiaeio oiieaeay
chy oeaiiar chaien tiay chiaaar saiied oiead chaay
oiin oieo saeey chieeir chieen taien teiad keaeo
kian qaeier cheey

shiien kied sieeid keaan tiey tiaial qaen kiiaad
siein qeiy qeieiy qy oeeo taiiio qaaiin deir
oeen teaeeen kiaao oiiad saiaaad tein qy oeiel
chaieien cheay dieeen chy sieeo oaiiy saiy daeeo
dieaeir tiay oiiad oial cheeaiir qeaiean oaey
saiy oaaeo seeal chiaeeio saeer oiiiar qiiy cthain
oeel oeieo chaeen chieaan qieaaer sy oeieo chaied
oaeo qiaeed cheiir chaien qy siaey seaeieo daiiy
oiiao oaail deiaaeo oaieiaey kiiiar keiiiio
qiaian chaeiiey sieaed taiiy sy siaey saeed
oeaaed qeeer ty oeaaen qaan oaiy oiael keeo
qaaeiio oian kaiein oain oeiy keaiaen chaiaiy chy
oaeeaeo oaiaey chy oaaaio chaao kaal seaiil sair
sy saaeao sair chaeiy ty cheaain chaean tiay chiaaar
chiien qaiied oiead diieiay oaid chaiiad qieen
keey

faiy teaeeen kieieey chaeeo dieaeir tiieo kian
qaaed chiiiiel keieaen qaiaaey cheeiid oeaen tiien
kied saiaiid qeeean sien cheiair oeiy kaain
keiieao tiil oeiao tiin taiian chiin chiar chaeaid
chiaeeiy seay qaiel keiio cheieil seey chiieeo kaao
qiiiir ky sieein teen oeaian sean chean oaain
keiieeo oaaeo chaiar tiey qaaean oeaen kaan qiein
qaeaed qaeiir qeeaaad oiiil oaiien daein chiaiy
oaey siaio chaaieeo qeeeao sir tiieeo kaiieeo
seieay qiar qaaeay seeein qiiaieo qiaeed cheiid
siaeo kaiael saen teair ky chiil siaio oeiin
oaieen kiar chaeaid chiaeeiy sieey saiar taeao
oeeaiio tiiieo char kaaiio qeaaiio chiaiey ty
cheeaan chiaiar qaer oeel chieeel oeaer keieo chaied
oiaaad siaeo kaiaey oeaeio kaaaaaey qeay keaen
qaid cheaeaad tiiien chy seel oeieo chaeen diar
oaan qeiy seeeiar tiieeo kaiieeo seieay qy oaey
tiien keiel oaeail tear oeieaey oiar daaed cheaeo
qeey siaen oy sien cheay deeeo chaeen deiio
seaaiil teaeaan teen siaein taiio qiaaad chiiir
taeeeo oeieay qiaey seir oeeo keiaaen ty oaaaean
chiar oeiiiio qiaian oeiio chaieiar qiail cheail
oiiieen qeean qaiier sy oiiad chieed oiio qeeaeey
oeiein siein tead oieieed kaaain chaeal chiaiieeo
chear oeiaair chy oeiaaid oeien qean oiiieo sair
saail qael oeaeiy saeay seir oiaeeir taieo chaied
oaaaer keiaeo cheeaiaiy oeey cthiin oieo saeey
chieeir saeid taiao tein qaeao oiio tiaiey ty
saer cheaeio qieil deaiain oiaal oial oaeiiao
cheiid chein qy cheiio siaeo qiaao chieiar teiaaer
kiin oieo saeey chiar saier qiaao oeaiaed teiaiil
oiaeo taer qaain keiio chaieiar qaid saeail
kaieiad qaen taay

faiy kaiiey kieieey cheir cheaaen daeael chiieial
oieain chiey taeal chiaiel qaen kiiaad siein qeiy
qiail seieay qy oaal chaeao siaan deir chiaen oy
chiil siaio oeiin oaeiiar qaid chaiiad tiiien chy
oeaiiar oy chaeao siaial keaio cheiid chear oeieo
keaen taer qiead daaaiiy qy chiiiain oiio cheeo
kiain teeeir qy oaey keaao chiaio kaal saaan seay
taer kiaao oaiid daeeo oaiaer qy siaey oiio cheeo
kiain teeeir qy siaail diad oiiin oeaaid tad
keial kieeeo oeen ty oiaeeed sair chieeen oaain
kiaeeed seieay qy chaiian kiin chied saiaiid
qeeeao oian kaiein oain oeel oeieo chaeen chaaiar
cheiio saiaiid taial oieaiy saeiiar qean oiaial
chiil sieain cheay diieiir qiiy

faiy kaaan keeid chiiail chaian tiey qaeiiid chein
kiiad cheeaeo teiaar qaaeed chaian ker qiiad
oaaeal oed tear seay kiaeeed saen teeid saaiao
qaeiir qeaaiir ckhaaiiad saaed chaiaey char kiaao
oiiad saiaaad tein kiear teen oeiel oieal
qeiiiaer chy siail cheail daean oiaad chaiiad
tiieiay oeaan qaeiiar qeiad oieey chiay chiar
oiaed qaaeer oeiay qeeaao qaiad oaeaeo qaey chy
cheeid seaad cheeo kieey sir qaiad chial cheaio
chaeio qeaeil chear oiaad saiel chiain tiay saan
chaiein cheen chaian kiiad seeeeeiio oiay cheaaen
qaan oaiy deiair deeiel oeaaiy saaear oaan
chaiein oain oiaal sair saein chiien taeil teaaao
chaan qy oeiio chaieiar kiaal oiiad dieial oier
saan oeiaen teiaead qaen qaaeao seieay tiay cheen
oaeaen taein chieiiay sy oeiil oier seen kiar
deiio cheeid oieiiid qaen qaan chaeal cheail
kaeeeiar oain chiaeil oieel cheiiy oaer saaao
siaan diaeien qy chiaaar oeieo chaied oiaaad siaeo
kaiaey siiy

faiy taaed oeiaen kiiiain kiar dieaeid kaiid
diiel cheeey cheiair deeiid oeaen kaey tiien
taeeeed kaaar taaeer seeaao chieir chiiao saaed
cheeaiaiy oieaal chiaeeio saeed saen ky seiy oiin
dieaen oeiaer keieiar kaey cheeael oaiaieo qaaen
oiin chaeian teeeeid chiiiar taaed cheaid oieey
oaaial oaaaer taaaad qael oeeeao oeaien chy siaay
chaer keeiir oiaen seeao tein qaeao chiaay oy
oiieil daaed oieil dieeay oaey oeial kieeeo oeen
qaieel oaeiiao cheiir saan chaeal cheail kaeeeiar
sy oieaal deiio chaeeed oier seaiead tein qy
chiien teieiy tiieiin chiay cheeio saeao qeeal
daaay chaeiy oied saiieo teiiy seiaen qiiy

faiy taaed oeiaen qeiein kiiaad siein keeid
daaeiy qeaan tain oiaear cheiay oaain kiiiair
kaal oiiaao chian ty siail cheail daeao sair cheeeo
chaeen oaiad chial cheeel saeial daiao qiein qiaeid
oieeey oiaen oy oeaeao dear oiiio oeiin daeian
taeiad saiiiy sir qy chiay chiar oiiio oeiin chieir
qeial qaeir qeeael oeaaiy chaaed deiy oy oaal
chaeao siaan oieaaer chaiaeo teaiiil oeen ty seel
oeieo chaeen diar oaaaaay qeen kiar cheiel saiao
qaen ky oeiiel chial chieeeiein chaeiy kaiaaao oied
chiaiey ty oaiier oiey chaey saiy oaiiir taaaad
keael daied cheain chieain qiiy

faiy tiiiaer kiaal siaieel oaeiir qeaiaed tieed
deeiir cheiair deeiid oeaen kaey teiil oeieaal
chiaeer ty sieeo deiaaeo oiaad oier seaeey qy
oaal chaeao siaan deay diin kaain keiein chy
oaaaio seaeeed daaieiey saaeal cheen qaain teeio
chaeaer sieiao qaieeel chaan qy oiiad chieed oiio
qeeaeey chiieer ty oeaaen qaan chaiein chaeil qiio
teeiaao saen tien chaal chaeao siaan oeean kieead
cheaid diiy oeaiiar seeaeen chaay

faiy kaiiey keaiail keeeer keaio cheaeeed kier
saaao siaan oeaiaed tieed oeeal daeeiy kiiaad
siein kaain keaiail keeir oieao oieiiid qair
chieaeid teeiel cheeey cheiair oeiy kiaad chieiaio
oiaaad sieaean chy chaeeal chaed saaan chaer ty
sieiey sy chiaaiao chieiay chiay chaaed oieil dieeay
oeaeio kieaaal siin ky seeear ty siail oeaiaio
oier seaaan sy chaeeeer tiar oiin oeaiiad chein qy
chaeeeeo keiein cheeid seaad cheeo kaaiaay saan
sieaay keaen qeil kiaao oaay chiiian qiar taeael
chaiil oeiieel deiaaeo oiaar chiieiir qiiy

faiy kaiiey qaaed oeiio cheiao kiieen chiiio oeiin
cheaio oeieil qieen qaaeed chaian keiiel chial
chieeeiein cheiid seaeieo cheiiid oeaiain cheiael
cheaal sieeid qiieaay qaain teiid saieeio qaiil
chaiaid seiieen kiieeeid deiaaeo oiaar cheiio
cheeid cheeaaal siin qeaeey teiio saeiaen qy
chaieian sieiey oaiin qy chieiiao chian qeel
chaeaiil deiaar kiaed saeid teaaao chaan qid taeil
teaaao chaan qy oiiad siieaan kaiiar chiien taieo
saiaaad tear oiaeien

faiy qiaein qiaio oeaid chiieeel siaeo kaiael
chaian qiiad oaaeal oeaen qaaeed chaian qeean
taeao siead oaaiiil chaeio qeaeil chear cheeiel sid
kier oean saeier cheiio chaieiar kaain keiieeo
oaaeo chaiar teean qaaed chiiiiel keieaen teean
qiaao oeaiaer qeean taeal seeaeio saeaer tiieeo
daiil siin teean qieeo oiiieey teean kaiaaad
dear cheen chiieio tein tieain kaiein chaaiiy
siaein kiiain cheen oaeaen kieiiay sy cheiil cheil
chiaeeio saeer oiiio oeiio deil daieo saeed
oeaiain oiaeeir qy oeiil oier cheaiin teian oaain
qiail oaaiaeo oiaiar ty oiin siail saiel
tiaaaeir seeeiar qy cheiil cheil chiaeeio saeed
sair chaeiy ty seiy teieo daao taieien saeay seir
chiiiaer kaeeer chy oiaal oial oaeiiao cheiid chear
saeid oiiar cheir oaaao siaao chial cheaio cheiid
sieaean chy seel oeieo chaeen deiio siaeo qiaao
chieiar teean teieo cheaan teean kaiaaad dear cheen
oeaian siead deiaar kiaed cheeir saer deieo daao
taieien oiin oieo saeey chiien tieeer qeieey kaan
qeiy seeeiar qaaear oaan chaiein oeey

faiy qiaein keiein cheaail daied chaiiey oiiaad
siein qeiy qaer cheiein oeeaay chaer taeear
qaaaean ty oeaiaen seeaao kaiied sair sy oeiel
saiel oiain oiey chaiiin oy oeiay siin qiaeein ky
cheair qieaaed oian kaey saaaaey seean qiaian
oieeal cheaid daeiear sy saiien siain qaeiaiy
dieeer qiiy

shaeein taieao chieiiid oair oaiy tiiiiiaao oaiid
deeeir qiiy ckhaaeed chaian tiay qeiieil chear siiy
cthain oaiy chiin daiiad keen qaaeeio kaeeer saiel
taaieiao cheiiy oeeid oeaien qy oaaiir cheir
oeaiean oaey saiy deir oaeeo seaad cheaead taien
oeial saen qiaaaao taeaen oaieel oier cheiad
oiaed qaiiio siin keir qiiieeo kieeeo oian kaan
qeean taeal chieed deeaaal seieay qy chaeiiey cheir
oeiieil chear oaaaaay qaaao siaan oaiar teeid
cheil daaieer ty chaeiy oeaer qy oaal chaeao siaan
deay daiaeo keiein seen tiieo kian keiieeo
daieal chaan qy siaail diad oiiin oiey cheiiiio
qiaian oaal chaeao siaan deay diin kaain qaid
oaaial chaieiar qieeo chial oaaed sieid tar ty
chaeiy cheiad kiiaar chaeio qaiaaad qiean seeaao
oiieaeay oieir qaaed chieaaao oiain qaeien qiael
qaiiio sieaiy saiy oaaaer taeal chaeieal qeeean
sy chaeaiio deil oaey oeean keiieio tieaeir
tiieiin oain oieeo deaiiy oeean qeaaeir
oaaaaaeeo seaad chaail oaiiiaian siiy

paaeed chaian teiair chaeeeo qiiar cheir tiil
oaiaaed kaaaeo qeeer qeieey qaeier cheey cthiiaad
siein teiaao qiiar oeaien tiieo kian qeel
chieiiar tiiiao kaaar kain oeiy kiaiay ty seaan
kaaiaiy oaiiair chaeaay teen chieiiid oair saan
chaiein oaiy keiain cheean qeay kiiiain qieo saeey
saan oeieey kaiaaad dear cheen oeaian oiaeiel
oiieaed qiio qiaaiar oeiiiay cheiein oieid deil
daieo siaeo kaiael sair oiieio qeey cheiaeal
cheeaiad keeo teail oiieaeay oaan keiaen tiiiao
kaaar keey ckhiein qeeeio tiiy oaeeen keiaair
saaed cheaeeed taaaar keian qaan chieeay kieal
tiieil deien teir chiiel tieed oiin qieeiay chaian
tiieial deeay keean siiad dieain qean oeiiio
taaer diin kiin oiaao chaeio qaiaaad qieao oiaad
oian keiaaid cheil chiiad chieed cheeeaan kaey oaey
oaieio saey oaeid teeiir seeaeer teiid chaial
cheieaeo kiay daio oeeir cheiian taian teiio siaeo
kieiiid qaeiiao cheiid chein taain oeeid oeaien
keey cphien oien seeao tein teeeo saieial cheiar
tiey kiieio tiiir teir chiieio tiiir tead oeeaiio
kian qeay qeiein taein kieiiay sy oaiy teieiy
kaey tiay chaieian seay taer qeiein ky seean
teiaiio tiaiey kiaiay kaey oaeeeo qiiar oeir
chaaiiy oaeaay kiar daieo chieed oiio tiiir ky
seeeen oaiiael qiaen qaain kaeiy kaiaaeo oaen
qeaien tiay chiaaao keeir chaieel oian kaiaay ky
seeeen oaeeaad daeiy kaiean teiair chaeeeo qiiar
deir oiiiiao oael oaaear kiaed qieio qaen tiey
keir tiaian qy chiien teaeaio tiiieo oeiaar
saeaey qieiao qaieeel chaan qieein dain chiieo
kian kiiain chiieiey sy chieeier diaiiy teir
oeaeeio dein taieo chieed oiio tiiir tiay qeean
teiaiio tiaiey kiiain chaeeain kaan taiil saey
daay ctheean chiil siaio oeiin oeaan qiean kean
saer chiaal oial oaeiiao cheiid chein tiieiir ty
seel oeieo chaeen daieey taien kain chiiiar qaer
chiaal oial oaeiiao cheiid chein qaail taein teir
oaeel sael deiaaeo oiaad oian keiiean taiiy chiin
taeian taeal cheail kaeeeiar sy sien chiey tiaian
tiien teeien oy chiey qaeed seaad oael oaiiiaian
chain chiien kiiiao chaail diiir qaeay oiin oieo
saeey cheeeo chaeen oiar keaid oaeel oeeo kiaer
kiar chiiad oieal oiar kiaear oeiiio taaer diien
qaaeeo oiead chaay seen qiar tiiiao kaaar kaeaay
kiar daieo chieed oiio tiiir qeiiio kieian tieeay
keiaed oieiiir keiao teaeeed taieo saeer siaen
kiar deiil cheil chiaeeio saeer oeaeio qieil
deaiaio cheiy oy chiaaan tiien keeid chiaio kian
kaiaio tieean oaeien keaaen diaaan qir teiael
cheain chieeen oeeeer qiead deiaar taeiier siar
keeid chiaio kian kaeeen tar keaio cheaeeed taieo
saeer oeeeer taiian dead oeeaiio kian qaaed
oaeaid oeiaear ty oeaiail qaeiiao cheiid cheeid
seiead diaeier qaeed seaaio oiiaiiy oy saeao
qeeal deeier teiad saaer saeien qaieay qaiy oeiy
seaiao cheiy oaied chaied oieied qaeo kieeen chaeil
teiy cheiaeo cheeeaan ty cheiaal daeao oieeeieial
cpheiar tiieil chiiiar qiaeear oeir qeeeio tiiir
qeiiin ker qieao oiaio keaal saen qiiiir keaaen
chied siieaao qaen teeaao deiaad qaiiio oeiaar
siiaar chaaeaio kian teiain qy chiaaaeo oeeeaar
kieir kiiio cheaiael oiaer qy cheaer taieo saeao
qieel chein kied siieaao qaen qaeeo oieaeil cheaiy
teiain kiar chied siieaao qaen taied deil chaeaio
saaed sieir kiaao cheiaein qaiel tiaaaeio saeiiao
sieier chy oaeeal oeaaiy oaal chiaied daaaar keey
cphien chied cheeiaan oieain teian chaer kiaao
cheaiael cheaeir qiaao oeaiaed qieeir tieed chaiael
oaaaail tein qy chaeiial deeay qaer oaid chaaieeo
tein qaein siar teiio saieial saiaer qaeien
qeaiear qaiar qeeeio tiiir keaaen diaaan qir
kaal seeeeal tieiiid oieaio oeaer qaeien qiein
qeiiin qieao oaiieel qaaeo char qiaen kiar chied
siieaao qaen ky oiead siead cheaead kian qaaed
saeao daiad qeaeiy chaein kaey oaeeen keieil
oeaal oial cheeeaan kaieair kieir qeel sieiey
oeaaen chied cheeiaan oieain taer qeel chieiiad
tiiiao kaaar taieir taaaar tieieo qeaain dead
oaao qaaen oieain ty saaed chaeaiil chian ty
oeaiail qaeiiao cheiid cheeid seiead diaeier
qeaaer oaain qaaaaay taiian taan keian qiien
saail kaaial chiaeer teiaan keaen kaiaeal sair
oiear keiaeo cheeeaan keaaen tiar oiaad oeael
deail kiaao oaay cheeid oieieen taer qeel sieiey
chien dieiaiy cheaien tiar saier keieil cheil
daaaiin keey cthy seiy oiead oaeeer oaiaio tieean
chied seeo teein seaaer siail siaao oeeeao oian
ty oaiaaad chial oeieao saeid qiin ky siael
qaiiio siin qaeed sieil chiiaeio cheiael deieal
qiaeer cheeio seaaean seiy saiean qeaeao kaiien
chiaad oeaid chial cheaay cheiad oaao qaaeo sieain
chien chied cheeiaan oieain qaer chiaao chaaieed
keain oiaed saeid teaaao chaao taieo saiaaar tien
daaain seiy chiil siaio chiiiiey teian oeeid diil
oeieiar qean dead oaiaey oieain qaer chieel
sieiel chaied oiaaar sieaer teeid oieieen keaio
cheaeeer qy siar teiid chaied cheaay sieiao qaieeel
chaan teiil oeiay qiein qeiy cheeid cheil daaieer
keaein tar qieeo diiy oeeid diil oeieiar kieaail
oiein kieal cheaien siin qieao oieiiir teian saer
chiaao cheaeeed kiaeeio saeer chy oiaeiel daeeo
daaeaay sir teiid chaied chiaao oeiiir oiiio oeiin
cheieiad teaaan siieey saiar kieal cheaien oieain
qeeein qiiad deiaar qy cheiaeay oiaio cheaeeed
kaay seeeeey qaein siar kaal oeaeay oaiaio
tieean chied seeo teein saaao oaiaaad oeaeeid
kieal daaieiey siaeeil oaaiy tiay oied siieaao
qaen kiead seaaid oeaan ky sieein qaiy oaeid
teeiir siael qaiiio siio kieal daaieiey seaien
taieo chieed oiaeair chien chiiad siaeo qaaeiid
oiiiir teir oiael qaiiio siio qaeir qeay kiaal
oeaear oeey cphien chied seaeiy seaeiy oeir tieed
chaaaein taaaar tiin kieir qeel chieiiad tiiiao
kaaar keey cthy seiy seiiir oer qeaaiir cpheaid
oeaid cheaed kiieeo deaaan cheay dieaain tieeer
qean oaaer seeeiar taaeer chy chaeiar qy chaaed
oeiaen kaiieer cthiaear daiio chieaay oaiied oiead
chaay saaeo oaiiain seay keaaed qiio qiaaiar
oaeed siead daaieir tiey qiaaiil diaaad oeen
qiaio cheaeeed qieen kaaiar oaiy taeeaid kaaad
tieaeid taien saeed siead daiiy oaiieer
